SYSTEMATIC SEARCH LOG
Scopus Search Protocol for 7 Literature Streams
DBA Thesis  |  Aiman S. Sadeq  |  SDA Bocconi  |  Supervisor: Professor Borgonovo
How to Use This Document
For each of the 7 streams, this document provides the exact Scopus search string, a results log table, and space for notes. Follow these steps for each stream:
1. Log in to Scopus using your SDA Bocconi institutional credentials.
2. Go to Advanced Search (not basic search).
3. Copy the exact search string from this document and paste it into the search box.
4. Record the date and total number of results in the log table.
5. Screen titles and abstracts. Record how many pass initial screening.
6. Export the screened results to your reference manager (Mendeley, Zotero, or EndNote).
7. Repeat for Web of Science and Google Scholar if needed.
8. Remove duplicates across databases. Record final count.
Quality Criteria (meet 3 of 5)
